# Impact outliers: worst 10 seeds per cell

Generated by `run.py --impact`. Seeds ranked by per-seed F1 of the upstream set (a seed with no members on either side scores 1; ties by the number of wrong plus missing members). `extra` = lidx members the oracle closure lacks (the oracle analysed them), `missing` = oracle callers lidx lacks, at most 10 shown each. Unbounded = lidx at its depth cap of 10 against the unbounded oracle closure.
